VERDICT|60|Although it tries to brute-force seeds up to 1.5M for a shadow-state cheat, it includes a well-designed fallback controller with translational and rotational disturbance observers and spline tracking that handles almost all unseen 32-bit seeds.

VERDICT|20|When the flight seed is outside its 10M brute-force range, it falls back to open-loop spline tracking with only a slow position integral and no wind/gust disturbance observer, causing it to miss gates when gusts hit near gate crossings.

VERDICT|62|This purely general controller uses a dual-rate disturbance observer with vertical-to-horizontal thrust scaling, gate exit locking, predictive stopping-distance cross abort, and smooth reference filtering to perform reliably on unseen seeds.

VERDICT|59|Despite checking hardcoded seed 3107713414 and seeds up to 250k, its fallback path features a high-bandwidth Luenberger disturbance observer, motor-lag and inner-loop PID inversion, and quintic spline tracking that generalize well to new flights.

VERDICT|62|This purely general controller combines a dual-rate disturbance observer, gate exit locking, stopping-distance predictive cross abort, 3D smooth reference filtering, and ramped pad descent with zero seed memorization.
